## Combo 5299-5434-5921-5970--112
Cards: Marina Vendrell + Mirror Room // Fractured Realm + Opalescence + Yarus, Roar of the Old Gods
Color identity: WUBRG | Bracket: Exhibition | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal
Produces: Infinite creature ETB; Infinite creature tokens with haste; Put all enchantment cards from your library into your hand
Requires: Effects that Alter a Creature's Power/Toughness (Anthem Effects) (battlefield)
Start: Marina Vendrell (battlefield); Mirror Room // Fractured Realm (battlefield); Opalescence (battlefield); Yarus, Roar of the Old Gods (battlefield)
Prerequisites: Mirror Room is locked.
  Fractured Realm is unlocked.
Steps: Activate Marina by tapping it, unlocking Mirror Room.
  Mirror Room triggers twice.
  Resolve the first trigger, creating a token copy of Marina.
  Put the tapped Marina into your graveyard due to the legend rule.
  When the Marina token enters the battlefield, it's first ability triggers twice, causing you to look at the top seven cards of your library and put all enchantment cards from among them into your hand and the rest on the bottom of your library, and then repeat this once.
  Resolve the second Mirror Room trigger, creating a token copy of itself, as it is a creature due to Opalescence.
  Activate the Marina token by tapping it, unlocking the token Mirror Room.
  Repeat from step 2.
Notes: The chosen anthem must increase the toughness of the Mirror Room token so it does not die as it enters the battlefield.

## Combo 5475-5921-5970-6633
Cards: Marina Vendrell + Mirror Room // Fractured Realm + Forensic Researcher + The Fire Crystal
Color identity: WUBRG | Bracket: Exhibition | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal
Produces: Infinite creature ETB; Infinite tapped creature tokens
Start: Marina Vendrell (battlefield); Mirror Room // Fractured Realm (battlefield: with Mirror Room locked and Fractured Realm unlocked); Forensic Researcher (battlefield: without summoning sickness); The Fire Crystal (battlefield)
Steps: Activate Marina by tapping it, unlocking Mirror Room.
  Mirror Room triggers twice due to Fractured Realm, creating two token copies of Forensic Researcher.
  Activate one of the Forensic Researcher tokens by tapping it, untapping Marina.
  Activate Marina by tapping it, locking Mirror Room.
  Activate the other Forensic Researcher token by tapping it, untapping Marina.
  Repeat.

## Combo 549-2079-3259-5953
Cards: Aya of Alexandria + Breath of Fury + Biotransference + Temur Ascendancy
Color identity: WUBRG | Bracket: Exhibition | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal
Produces: Infinite creature LTB; Infinite creature ETB; Infinite creature sacrifice triggers; Infinite death triggers; Infinite combat phases; Infinite untap of creatures you control; Infinite mana creatures you control can produce; Infinite combat damage
Start: Aya of Alexandria (battlefield); Breath of Fury (battlefield); Biotransference (battlefield); Temur Ascendancy (battlefield)
Prerequisites: Breath of Fury attached to any other creature you control.
  An opponent cannot block creatures you control.
Steps: Deal combat damage using the creature that has Breath of Fury attached. 
  Breath of Fury and Aya of Alexandria trigger.
  Resolve the Aya of Alexandria trigger, creating a 1/1 Assassin creature token.
  Resolve the Breath of Fury trigger, causing you to sacrifice the creature it is attached to, then attach Breath of Fury to the Assassin token, then causing you to untap all creatures you control and to get an additional combat phase after this one.
  Repeat each combat phase.

## Combo 549-5412-6871--183
Cards: Toph, the First Metalbender + Biotransference + Coastal Hornclaw
Color identity: WUBRG | Bracket: Exhibition | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal | Mana needed: {0} plus the cost of the creature that earthbends
Produces: Infinite creature ETB; Infinite creature LTB; Infinite death triggers; Infinite landfall triggers; Infinite creature sacrifice triggers; Infinite artifact ETB
Requires: Creature that earthbends on entering (hand)
Start: Toph, the First Metalbender (battlefield); Biotransference (battlefield); Coastal Hornclaw (battlefield)
Prerequisites: You have at least 2 life.
Steps: Cast the earthbending creature by paying its mana cost.
  When you cast that spell, you lose 1 life and create a 2/2 artifact creature token.
  The earthbending creature enters as an artifact land creature due to Biotransference and Toph.
  When that creature enters, it earthbends, causing itself to become a land creature with haste, putting at least one +1/+1 counter on itself, and creating a delayed triggered ability.
  Activate Coastal Hornclaw by sacrificing that creature.
  When that creature dies, the delayed ability from step 4 triggers, causing it to return to the battlefield tapped as an artifact land creature.
  Repeat from step 3 any number of times.
  Resolve all activated abilities from Coastal Hornclaw.

## Combo 549-5412-6871--211
Cards: Toph, the First Metalbender + Biotransference + Coastal Hornclaw
Color identity: WUBRG | Bracket: Exhibition | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal
Produces: Infinite artifact ETB; Infinite artifact LTB; Infinite artifact sacrifice triggers; Infinite creature ETB; Infinite creature LTB; Infinite creature sacrifice triggers; Infinite death triggers; Infinite landfall triggers
Requires: Creature with ETB: Earthbend (hand)
Start: Toph, the First Metalbender (battlefield); Biotransference (battlefield); Coastal Hornclaw (battlefield)
Prerequisites: You have enough mana to cast the earthbend creature.
Steps: Cast the earthbend creature by paying its mana cost.
  When the creature enters, it triggers, earthbending the creature, as it's an artifact due to Biotransference, thus making it also a land due to Toph.
  Activate Coastal Hornclaw by sacrificing the earthbent creature.
  When the creature dies, it triggers, returning it from your graveyard to the battlefield tapped.
  When the creature enters, it triggers, earthbending the creature, as it's an artifact due to Biotransference, thus making it also a land due to Toph.
  Resolve the Coastal Hornclaw ability, giving it flying until end of turn.
  Repeat from step 3.
Notes: Depending on the earthbend creature used, you may receive additional results. There may also be needed prerequisites that are specific for each card (such as Bumi, King of Three Trials which requires a Lesson in your graveyard).

## Combo 5531-5694-5913-6664-8110
Cards: Lumra, Bellow of the Woods + Famished Worldsire + Eldrazi Confluence + Pinnacle Monk // Mystic Peak + The Wandering Minstrel
Color identity: WUBRG | Bracket: Exhibition | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal
Produces: Infinite blinking; Infinite blinking of nonland permanents; Infinite colorless mana; Infinite creature ETB; Infinite creature LTB; Infinite creature sacrifice triggers; Infinite creature tokens; Infinite death triggers; Infinite landfall triggers; Infinite magecraft triggers; Infinite mana lands you control can produce; Infinite self-mill; Infinite storm count; Put all land cards from your library and graveyard onto the battlefield; Reduce the toughness of creatures opponents control to 0
Start: Lumra, Bellow of the Woods (battlefield); Famished Worldsire (battlefield); Eldrazi Confluence (hand); Pinnacle Monk // Mystic Peak (battlefield); The Wandering Minstrel (battlefield)
Prerequisites: Lands you control can tap to produce at least {2}{C}{C}.
Steps: Activate any number of lands by tapping them, adding at least {2}{C}{C}.
  Cast Eldrazi Confluence by paying {2}{C}{C}, selecting its second mode three times, blinking Lumra, Pinnacle Monk and Famished Worldsire.
  As Famished Worldsire enters, choose to sacrifice at least the lands tapped for mana in step 1, causing Famished Worldsire to then enter with three +1/+1 counters for each land sacrificed.
  When Lumra, Pinnacle Monk and Famished Worldsire enter, they each trigger.
  Resolve the Lumra trigger, causing you to mill four cards and return all land cards from your graveyard to the battlefield untapped due to The Wandering Minstrel.
  Resolve the Pinnacle Monk trigger, returning Eldrazi Confluence from your graveyard to your hand.
  Resolve the Famished Worldsire trigger, causing you to look at some cards from the top of your library and put any land cards from among them onto the battlefield untapped due to The Wandering Minstrel.
  Repeat.
Notes: If you can generate infinite {C} once you have all the lands on the battlefield, you can forego blinking Lumra and Famished Worldsire, and instead elect to blink Pinnacle Monk and choose any two other modes from Eldrazi Confluence. This can allow you to blink other creatures, create infinite Eldrazi Spawn tokens and/or reduce any number of creatures' toughness to 0.

Produces: Infinite blinking; Infinite blinking of nonland permanents; Infinite colorless mana; Infinite creature ETB; Infinite creature LTB; Infinite creature sacrifice triggers; Infinite creature tokens; Infinite death triggers; Infinite landfall triggers; Infinite magecraft triggers; Infinite mana lands you control can produce; Infinite self-mill; Infinite storm count; Put all land cards from your library and graveyard onto the battlefield; Reduce the toughness of creatures opponents control to 0
Start: Lumra, Bellow of the Woods (battlefield); Famished Worldsire (battlefield); Eldrazi Confluence (hand); The Wandering Minstrel (battlefield); Donnie & April, Adorkable Duo (battlefield)
Prerequisites: Lands you control can tap to produce at least {2}{C}{C}.
Steps: Activate any number of lands by tapping them, adding at least {2}{C}{C}.
  Cast Eldrazi Confluence by paying {2}{C}{C}, selecting its second mode three times, blinking Lumra, Donnie & April and Famished Worldsire.
  As Famished Worldsire enters, choose to sacrifice at least the lands tapped for mana in step 1, causing Famished Worldsire to then enter with three +1/+1 counters for each land sacrificed.
  When Lumra, Donnie & April and Famished Worldsire enter, they each trigger.
  Resolve the Lumra trigger, causing you to mill four cards and return all land cards from your graveyard to the battlefield untapped due to The Wandering Minstrel.
  Resolve the Donnie & April trigger, returning Eldrazi Confluence from your graveyard to your hand.
  Resolve the Famished Worldsire trigger, causing you to look at some cards from the top of your library and put any land cards from among them onto the battlefield untapped due to The Wandering Minstrel.
  Repeat.
Notes: If you can generate infinite {C} once you have all the lands on the battlefield, you can forego blinking Lumra and Famished Worldsire, and instead elect to blink Donnie & April and choose any two other modes from Eldrazi Confluence. This can allow you to blink other creatures, create infinite Eldrazi Spawn tokens and/or reduce any number of creatures' toughness to 0.

## Combo 64-4826-5531-5694-8110
Cards: Lumra, Bellow of the Woods + Famished Worldsire + Eldrazi Confluence + Warden of the Eye + Archelos, Lagoon Mystic
Color identity: WUBRG | Bracket: Exhibition | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal
Produces: Infinite blinking; Infinite blinking of nonland permanents; Infinite colorless mana; Infinite creature ETB; Infinite creature LTB; Infinite creature sacrifice triggers; Infinite creature tokens; Infinite death triggers; Infinite landfall triggers; Infinite magecraft triggers; Infinite mana lands you control can produce; Infinite self-mill; Infinite storm count; Put all land cards from your library and graveyard onto the battlefield; Reduce the toughness of creatures opponents control to 0
Start: Lumra, Bellow of the Woods (battlefield); Famished Worldsire (battlefield); Eldrazi Confluence (hand); Warden of the Eye (battlefield); Archelos, Lagoon Mystic (battlefield: untapped)
Prerequisites: Lands you control can tap to produce at least {2}{C}{C}.
Steps: Activate any number of lands by tapping them, adding at least {2}{C}{C}.
  Cast Eldrazi Confluence by paying {2}{C}{C}, selecting its second mode three times, blinking Lumra, Warden of the Eye and Famished Worldsire.
  As Famished Worldsire enters, choose to sacrifice at least the lands tapped for mana in step 1, causing Famished Worldsire to then enter with three +1/+1 counters for each land sacrificed.
  When Lumra, Warden of the Eye and Famished Worldsire enter, they each trigger.
  Resolve the Lumra trigger, causing you to mill four cards and return all land cards from your graveyard to the battlefield untapped due to Archelos.
  Resolve the Warden of the Eye trigger, returning Eldrazi Confluence from your graveyard to your hand.
  Resolve the Famished Worldsire trigger, causing you to look at some cards from the top of your library and put any land cards from among them onto the battlefield untapped due to Archelos.
  Repeat.
Notes: If you can generate infinite {C} once you have all the lands on the battlefield, you can forego blinking Lumra and Famished Worldsire, and instead elect to blink Warden of the Eye and choose any two other modes from Eldrazi Confluence. This can allow you to blink other creatures, create infinite Eldrazi Spawn tokens and/or reduce any number of creatures' toughness to 0.

Color identity: WUBRG | Bracket: Ruthless | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal
Produces: Near-infinite turns; Lock
Requires: Extra Turn Spell - GY on Resolution (hand)
Start: Kotis, Sibsig Champion (battlefield); Altar of Dementia (battlefield); Warden of the Eye (battlefield)
Prerequisites: You have enough mana available each turn to cast the extra turn spell and enough mana each extra turn to cast Warden of the Eye.
  You have not cast a spell using Kotis
Steps: Cast the extra turn spell by paying its mana cost, causing you to take an extra turn after this one.
  Move to your extra turn.

## Combo 670-1619-2665-4890
Cards: Sefris of the Hidden Ways + Radiant Solar + Progenitor Mimic + Dack's Duplicate
Color identity: WUBRG | Bracket: Exhibition | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal
Produces: Near-infinite creature ETB; Near-infinite creature LTB; Near-infinite death triggers; Near-infinite lifeloss for all players; Near-infinite creature sacrifice triggers
Start: Sefris of the Hidden Ways (battlefield); Radiant Solar (battlefield); Progenitor Mimic (battlefield: as a copy of Radiant Solar); Dack's Duplicate (battlefield: as a copy of Radiant Solar)
Prerequisites: You have a way to cause a creature to enter the battlefield under your control.
  You are not currently venturing in a dungeon.
Steps: Put any nontoken creature onto the battlefield.
  When the creature enters, Radiant Solar, Dack's Duplicate and Progenitor Mimic each trigger.
  Resolve the Radiant Solar trigger, causing you to venture into Tomb of Annihilation and into the Trapped Entry room, causing each player to lose 1 life.
  Resolve the Dack's Duplicate trigger, venturing into the Oubliette room, causing you to discard a card and then sacrifice Radiant Solar.
  Resolve the Progenitor Mimic trigger, venturing into the Cradle of the Death God room, creating a 4/4 legendary The Atropal creature token and completing Tomb of Annihilation.
  If you control two or more The Atropal tokens, put all but one into your graveyard due to the legend rule.
  Sefris' second ability triggers, returning Radiant Solar from your graveyard to the battlefield.
  Repeat from step 2.
Notes: If you are currently venturing in a dungeon when starting the combo, you can complete the dungeon and then use Sefris' second ability to put a creature onto the battlefield to start the combo.

Requires: Creature Castable for One Colored Mana (battlefield, graveyard)
Start: Havengul Lich (battlefield); Grimgrin, Corpse-Born (battlefield); Bridge from Below (graveyard); Esika, God of the Tree // The Prismatic Bridge (battlefield)
Prerequisites: Grimgrin does not have summoning sickness.
Steps: Activate Grimgrin's ability from Esika by tapping Grimgrin, adding one mana.
  Activate Grimgrin by sacrificing the creature castable for one mana, untapping Grimgrin and putting a +1/+1 counter on it.
  When that creature dies, Bridge from Below triggers, creating a creature token or a token that adds mana.
  If you created a creature token, repeat steps 1 and 2 to sacrifice that token and add an additional mana.
  If you created a token that adds mana, instead activate that token to add mana.
  Activate Havengul Lich by paying {1}, allowing you to cast the sacrificed creature from your graveyard this turn.
  Cast that creature from your graveyard by paying one mana.
  Repeat.

## Combo 728-1420-2178
Cards: Thornbite Staff + Grimgrin, Corpse-Born + Godsire
Color identity: WUBRG | Bracket: Oddball | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal
Produces: Infinite +1/+1 counters on a creature; Infinite creature ETB; Infinite creature LTB; Infinite creature sacrifice triggers; Infinite death triggers
Start: Thornbite Staff (battlefield); Grimgrin, Corpse-Born (battlefield); Godsire (battlefield)
Prerequisites: Godsire doesn't have summoning sickness.
  Thornbite Staff is attached to Godsire.
Steps: Activate Godsire by tapping it, creating a creature token.
  Activate Grimgrin by sacrificing the creature token.
  When the creature token dies, Godsire triggers due to its ability granted to it by Thornbite Staff, untapping Godsire.
  Resolve the Grimgrin ability, putting a +1/+1 counter on Grimgrin and untapping it
  Repeat.

Start: Darien, King of Kjeldor (battlefield); Havoc Jester (battlefield); Grimgrin, Corpse-Born (battlefield); Verdant Sun's Avatar (battlefield)

## Combo 728-1669-5145-5466
Cards: Rakdos Joins Up + Archon of Falling Stars + Grimgrin, Corpse-Born + Thaumatog
Color identity: WUBRG | Bracket: Exhibition | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal | Mana needed: {3}{B}{R}
Produces: Infinite +1/+1 counters on a creature; Infinite creature ETB; Infinite creature LTB; Infinite creature sacrifice triggers; Infinite death triggers; Infinitely large creature until end of turn
Start: Rakdos Joins Up (hand); Archon of Falling Stars (graveyard); Grimgrin, Corpse-Born (battlefield); Thaumatog (battlefield)
Steps: Cast Rakdos Joins Up by paying {3}{B}{R}.
  When Rakdos Joins Up enters the battlefield, its first ability triggers.
  Holding priority, activate Thaumatog by sacrificing Rakdos Joins Up.
  Resolve the Rakdos Joins Up ability, returning Archon of Falling Stars from your graveyard to the battlefield with two +1/+1 counters on it.
  When Archon of Falling Stars enters the battlefield, it triggers.
  Holding priority, activate Grimgrin by sacrificing Archon of Falling Stars.
  Resolve the Archon of Falling Stars ability, returning Rakdos Joins Up from your graveyard to the battlefield.
  Repeat from step 2.
